### Round 12 — compaction lives in the compaction plugin, not core

**Accepted as delivered:**

- Round 11's Principle-1 violation is resolved: the full compaction algorithm (threshold resolve, plan build, tail-start find, preserve-before, summary insert/sync, summariseWithModel) now lives in `packages/poe-agent/src/plugins/poe-agent-plugin-compaction.ts`. `IterationContext.compact` and `AcpModel.contextWindow` are gone from the core surface (`plugin-types.ts` / `types.ts` / `acp-core.ts` all clean — the only `contextWindow` left is the plugin's own option type).
- New narrow iteration primitives landed on `IterationContext`: `ctx.complete(...)` (calls the run's model) and `ctx.runHook(event, ctx)` (dispatches pre/postCompaction). Both are plugin-agnostic — any future plugin that owns its own hook pair can reuse them.
- `packages/poe-agent/src/plugins/poe-agent-plugin-compaction.test.ts` exercises the plugin end-to-end through `runAcpCore`: default model-driven summary, `preCompaction: "skip"` bypass, custom `summarise(messages)`, and `contextWindow`-derived threshold. Full `@poe-code/poe-agent` suite passes (10 files / 178 tests); `tsc --noEmit` clean.

**Driving rework (real CLAUDE.md violation introduced by this round):**

- `resolveCompactionThreshold` (`poe-agent-plugin-compaction.ts:87‑104`) and `normalizeKeepLastTurns` (`:106‑112`) defensively re-validate `typeof x === "number" && Number.isFinite(x) && x >= 0` on `IterationCompactionOptions.threshold` / `.keepLastTurns` / `.contextWindow`. These fields are typed `number | undefined` — the plugin author owns the config and TypeScript already enforces the type. CLAUDE.md: _"Do not add error handling, fallbacks, or validation for scenarios that can't happen. Trust internal code and framework guarantees. Only validate at system boundaries (user input, external APIs)."_ Scope: collapse both helpers to `??`-based defaulting inline (e.g. `const window = options.contextWindow ?? DEFAULT_CONTEXT_WINDOW; const threshold = options.threshold ?? Math.floor(window * DEFAULT_THRESHOLD_RATIO);` and `const keep = options.keepLastTurns ?? DEFAULT_KEEP_LAST_TURNS;`); drop the `Number.isFinite` / `Math.trunc` / `Math.max(1, …)` guards. Keep test coverage green — the three existing threshold/`keepLastTurns` assertions must still pass unchanged.

**Rejected as scope creep (do not rework this round):**

- Collapse `HookRegistry` + the 10 `createXHookContext` factories into a data-driven `Map<HookEvent, Hook[]>` (inspector's Must-fix #1 and #2). Trigger set in Round 10 / reconfirmed in Round 11 was explicit: _"an 11th hook event, or a concrete feature that wants dynamic hook-name registration"_. Round 12 added zero hook events — it moved compaction into a plugin using the existing hook surface. The trigger still has not fired. The factories are also not pure proxies: they split public context fields from the internal `disposeRun` WeakMap channel — not "functions that do nothing just proxy". Revisit only when the trigger actually fires.
- Route tool-execution errors through `ToolResultErrorPart` + `toToolMessageContent` in `acp-core.ts:562` (inspector's Must-fix #3). Weak analysis: structured `{ type: "error", code, message, retriable }` values returned by a tool already travel via the success branch (`ack.status === "success"` → `mutableOutcome.result` → `toToolMessageContent` at `:538`), so that path is already structured. The `:562` branch only runs for synchronous throws and `preToolUse` reject / skip reasons, which are strings from inception and can't carry retry codes. Round 12 did not regress this path — it's Round 6 shape, already signed off. Not a solidness gap introduced by this round.
- Three tangential nits: `TurndownService` module-load instantiation (`poe-agent-plugin-web.ts:24-27`); private `isObjectRecord` in `tool-results.ts:3` vs. exported in `plugin-args.ts:3`; `getOptionalGrepOutputMode` one-off vs. a generic `getOptionalEnum<T>`. All pre-existing, all one-liners, all rejected consistently across Rounds 4/6/7/8 as cosmetic.
- `COMPACTION_MESSAGE_NAME` constant and `formatCompactionSummary` one-line helper. The name constant is a genuine marker used to preserve/sync the summary message across iterations — business logic, not concatenation. `formatCompactionSummary` is used 3× and keeps formatting consistent between initial insertion and post-hook sync. Neither is a CLAUDE.md "overuse of constants" / proxy-function violation.
- Plugin-name convention drift. Explicitly punted every round since 2.

**Testing inspector:** full suite (10 files / 178 tests) passes; direct coverage for the compaction plugin plus indirect coverage for `ctx.runHook` via the pre/postCompaction paths. No testing-side gaps worth driving rework. Direct `ctx.runHook` assertion can wait for a second plugin consumer.

**Status:** rework in flight (single task-board item: drop the defensive validators). **Owner review deferred** until the validators are gone.

### Round 11 — new plugin `poe-agent-plugin-compaction`

**Accepted as delivered (behaviour):**

- `poe-agent-plugin-compaction` ships; full `packages/poe-agent` suite (10 files / 177 tests) and `tsc --noEmit` green.
- Three new plugin tests cover the end-to-end behaviour through `runAcpCore`: default model-driven summary, `preCompaction: "skip"` bypass, and a custom `summarise(messages)` override.
- Threshold default, "keep last N user turns verbatim", single-system-note replacement of older context, and `preCompaction` / `postCompaction` hook dispatch all work as specified.

**Driving rework (real Principle-1 violation):**

- The "plugin" at `packages/poe-agent/src/plugins/poe-agent-plugin-compaction.ts` is a 3-line proxy (`postIteration(ctx) { await ctx.compact(options); }`) while ~200 LOC of algorithm — `createIterationCompactionRunner`, `resolveCompactionThreshold`, `buildCompactionPlan`, `findCompactionTailStart`, `shouldPreserveBeforeCompaction`, `insertCompactionSummaryMessage`, `createCompactionSummaryMessage`, `syncCompactionSummaryMessage`, `formatCompactionSummary`, `resolveCompactionSummary`, `summariseWithModel` (`packages/poe-agent/src/runtime/acp-core.ts:656‑898`) — lives in core, plus `IterationContext.compact` and `AcpModel.contextWindow` as new core surface. That directly contradicts the plan's Principle 1 ("Every feature ships as a plugin — the core stays minimal") and CLAUDE.md's "functions that do nothing just proxy to another functions are not allowed". No other built-in plugin (files, shell, web, spawn, skills) is this empty.
- Scope: (1) move the algorithm into `poe-agent-plugin-compaction.ts`; (2) delete `IterationContext.compact` from `plugin-types.ts` / `hooks.ts` / `acp-core.ts`; (3) delete `AcpModel.contextWindow` — the plugin can accept `threshold` / `contextWindow` as options or sniff it from the model wrapper inside the plugin if needed; (4) give the plugin whatever narrow primitive it needs to dispatch `preCompaction` / `postCompaction` (e.g. expose `ctx.runHook(event, ctx)` on `IterationContext`, reused by any future plugin that owns its own hook pair — not a compaction-specific method); (5) keep the existing test contracts green (plugin-level behaviour asserts stay; any core-runtime assertions about `ctx.compact` move or are deleted).

**Rejected as scope creep (do not rework this round):**

- Collapse `HookRegistry` + the 10 `createXHookContext` factories into a table-driven `Map<HookEvent, HookFn[]>`. Round 10 set the trigger explicitly: "an 11th hook event, or a concrete feature that wants dynamic hook-name registration". Round 11 added zero hook events (all six Round 10 hooks were consumed, none added) and no dynamic registration feature — trigger has not fired. Inspector's "six hooks in one round IS the trigger" argument doesn't hold: the trigger is forward-looking on the 11th event, not retroactive on the first 10. Cost of the refactor is a whole-registry rewrite plus compile-check churn for no solidness win.
- `hookContextDisposers` WeakMap side-channel. Pre-existing (Round 10), cosmetic, builder extended it consistently; already rejected in Round 10.
- `syncSubmittedUserPrompt` string-identity inference in `acp-core.ts:900‑911`. Pre-existing, orthogonal to Round 11's compaction task. Would benefit from a cleaner hook contract, but not a solidness gap introduced by this round.
- Plugin-name convention drift (`spawn`, `audit-log`, `environment`, `git-context`, `max-iterations`, `scratchpad`, `skills` vs. `poe-agent-plugin-<topic>`). Explicitly punted in Rounds 2, 3, 4, 7, 8, 9, 10 as cosmetic. Unchanged.
- Duplicates: `isObjectRecord` in `tool-results.ts:3` vs. `plugin-args.ts:3`; `normalizeActiveSkills` in `tools.ts:40` vs. `run-context.ts:17`. Pre-existing, cosmetic one-liners; rejected in Rounds 4, 6, 7, 8.
- Dead bookkeeping: `RunContext.trackChildRun` / `childRuns` / `getChildRunCount`. Pre-existing, untouched this round; rejected in Round 10.
- `__legacyAutoHandleTools` BC-shim flag on `AgentRunOptions`. Pre-existing, untouched this round; rejected in Round 10 with the note that it belongs in a dedicated task-board item if it matters.

**Testing inspector:** full suite (10 files / 177 tests) passes; every Round 11 branch — default, skip, custom `summarise` — has coverage. No testing-side gaps worth driving rework.

**Status:** rework in flight (see Task Board). **Owner review deferred** until the algorithm lives in the plugin.

### Round 10 — extend `AgentPlugin` hooks (sessionStart, userPromptSubmit, preCompaction, postCompaction, notification, stop)

**Accepted as delivered:**

- `AgentPlugin["hooks"]` + `HookEvent` + `HookContext` surfaces now include all six new events; registry storage, context factories, and `HookRegistry.copyFrom` cover them uniformly.
- Runtime execution wired for the three universal lifecycle hooks the core controls: `sessionStart` (before first iteration), `userPromptSubmit` (mutable — rewrites the prompt used by prompt compilation / model requests), and `stop` (final veto on run exit with finalization semantics).
- `preCompaction` / `postCompaction` / `notification` are registered and context-constructible but not invoked by the core. Correct — those fire from downstream plugins (compaction plugin = next task-board item; notification from long-running tools like background shell). No speculative wiring.
- Compile-check files and runtime tests exercise every new hook path. `npx vitest run packages/poe-agent/src` + e2e configs + `tsc --noEmit` all green.

**Rejected as scope creep (do not rework this round):**

- Collapse `HookRegistry` + the 10 `createXHookContext` factories into a table-driven `Map<HookEvent, HookFn[]>`. Real simplification opportunity (registry now has 10 private fields / 10 add-branches / 10 run-cases), but the boilerplate grew by following the existing pattern that every prior round accepted. Tests pass, the contract is correct — this is a refactor, not a solidness gap. Trigger for revisit: an 11th hook event, or a concrete feature that wants dynamic hook-name registration.
- `hookContextDisposers` WeakMap side-channel. Pre-existing pattern; builder extended it consistently. Folding it into a `disposeRun` field on `HookContext` is cosmetic.
- `__legacyAutoHandleTools` underscore-prefixed flag on `AgentRunOptions` (inspector flagged as CLAUDE.md BC-shim violation). Real concern but pre-existing, untouched by Round 10, and orthogonal to the hooks surface. Belongs in a dedicated task-board item if it matters.
- `RunContext.trackChildRun` / `childRuns` / `getChildRunCount` dead bookkeeping. Pre-existing, not introduced by this round.
- Plugin dependency graph (`resolvePluginSetupOrder`), deep-clone + `Object.freeze` in `config.ts`, `ImmutableAgentBuilder` immutability, two-fork paths (`createForkRunner` vs `AgentHost.fork`), `normalizeActiveSkills` triplication, `cwd` + `allowedPaths` bootstrapping duplication between files/shell plugins, three `appendSystem`-style plugins sharing the same pattern. All pre-existing, all flagged as "could be simplified" not "is broken". Rejected collectively as scope creep — they span almost the entire package and the builder did not introduce any of them.
- Plugin-name convention drift (`poe-agent-plugin-<topic>` vs short names). Explicitly punted in Rounds 2, 3, 4, 7, 8, 9 as cosmetic. Unchanged.
- `maxIterations` plugin vs. `AgentRunOptions.maxIterations` double-enforcement. Pre-existing; the plugin was not touched this round.

**Testing inspector:** full `@poe-code/poe-agent` suite (9 files / 174 tests) passes; every Round 10 line has corresponding assertions (runtime.test.ts:838,1019,1198,2652,2695 + compile-check files). No gaps worth driving rework.

**Owner review requested.**

  - the default ripgrep path forwards the same signal into `execFile`

Real solidness gap but pre-existing (Round 6 decision) and orthogonal to Round 7's ripgrep cancellation task. Added as a dedicated task-board item instead of being silently patched in this round.
- Private copy of `isObjectRecord` in `runtime/tool-results.ts` vs. the exported one in `plugin-args.ts`. The exported-helper question was already debated in Round 4 and rejected as "micro-style, not a solidness gap"; the Round 6 new file preserved the same local pattern. Same rationale still applies — cosmetic, one-liner, no behavior impact.
- Plugin-name string inconsistency (`poe-agent-plugin-<x>` vs. short names). Explicitly punted in Rounds 2, 3, 4 — cosmetic, does not affect solidness.

- MCP error double-wrap when non-text parts go through `toolResultPartToText`. Inspector explicitly scoped as "not breaking, worth tightening once more MCP servers return structured errors" — not a current gap.
- Missing `str_replace` error-path test for "match not unique + replace_all=false". Minor coverage gap; existing tests cover the path + happy branches. Fold in when `edit_file` is next touched.
- Background-spawn tests still exercise real `child_process`. Pre-existing flakiness concern deferred since Round 3 with the same "revisit when background grows streaming notifications" trigger; trigger has not fired.

### Round 6 — `Tool.call` returns `ToolResult` (text / image / error)

**Accepted as delivered:**

- `Tool.call` return type tightened to `ToolResult`; normalization threaded through runtime, ACP core, legacy `AgentSession` adapter, and Poe provider request serialization (image parts now map to provider image blocks).
- MCP tool bridging preserves multimodal results and returns structured MCP errors.
- `read_file` returns a structured image part when the target file is an image mime type.
- `ToolResult` / `ToolResultPart` re-exported from the package root.

**Driving rework (real solidness gaps flagged by inspectors):**

- Tightened `Tool.call`'s return type broke four call sites that only vitest missed because type-checking is skipped there. Fixed so `tsc --noEmit -p packages/poe-agent/tsconfig.json` is green:
  - `agent-session.ts:261` — `content` field added to the `@poe-code/agent-spawn` ACP-shim `ToolCallUpdate` (with a minimal `ToolCallContent` text/image union). The ACP spec already defines it; the shim just omitted it.
  - `agent.ts:411-413` — Poe provider JSON parse cast narrowed to an inline `{ content?: string | null; … }` shape. The old cast to `AcpModelRequestMessage` wrongly widened `content` to include `ToolResultPart[]`, which is an _outbound_ request shape never returned by the provider.
  - `poe-agent-plugin-files.ts:310` — `read_file` image branch annotated with `type: "image" as const` so the literal narrows to `ToolResultImagePart`.
  - `poe-agent-plugin-spawn.ts:18` — `ctx.spawn()` returns `RunOutput`, not a `ToolResult`. Wrapped with `JSON.stringify` (preserves the content string the model sees via `normalizeToolResult`, only the raw `toolCalls[].result` now holds the stringified payload; `plugins.test.ts` and `agent.spawn-mcp.runtime.e2e.test.ts` expectations updated to match).
- Duplicate `toolResultPartToText` collapsed: `runtime/plugin-api-impl.ts` now imports the shared helper from `runtime/tool-results.ts`. The private copy diverged on the `error` branch (returned `part.message` vs. the shared helper's `JSON.stringify(part)`), causing the same error part to format two ways between the MCP error path and the provider / size-estimation paths.
- `fileExists` in the files plugin now uses `fs.stat` instead of reading the whole file as UTF-8. The old impl threw on binary files (so `create` would silently succeed on a pre-existing image) and was wasteful for large files.

**Validation:** `npx tsc -p packages/poe-agent/tsconfig.json --noEmit` is clean; `npx vitest run packages/poe-agent` → 7 files / 166 tests pass.

**Rejected as scope creep (do not rework this round):**

- Export built-in plugins from `packages/poe-agent/src/index.ts`. Real DX gap (inspector flagged `agent().use(...)` users can't reach the tool surface without deep imports), but pre-existing across all rounds and orthogonal to the Round 6 multimodal-tool-result task. Added as a dedicated task-board item instead of being silently patched in this round.
- Collapse `filesPlugin` DI surface (`searchContent` / `globFiles`). Already rejected twice (Rounds 3 & 4) as style cleanup, not a solidness gap; both seams are tested.
- `systemPromptPlugin` `startsWith` dedupe. Minor robustness issue, pre-existing, not introduced by this round.
- Background-spawn tests using real `child_process`. Pre-existing flakiness concern raised in Round 3 but deferred with "revisit when background grows streaming notifications."
- Extract a single typed `getOptional` helper from the 6 `getOptional*` variants in `plugin-args.ts`. Explicit deferral — revisit only if a 7th variant shows up.

**Owner review requested.**

### Round 5 — `fetch_url` (HTML → markdown, paginated)

**Accepted as delivered:**

- `poe-agent-plugin-web` now exposes `fetch_url` alongside `search_web`.
- `fetch_url` performs HTTP GET, converts HTML / XHTML responses to markdown with `turndown`, and paginates output with an `offset`.
- Unit tests cover HTML → markdown conversion and pagination; the package vitest run now passes and `tsc --noEmit` passes for `packages/poe-agent`.

**Driving rework (solidness gap flagged by code-quality inspector):**

- `fetch_url` now takes `ctx: { signal }` and forwards it to `fetch(url, { signal })`. Without this, an aborted run leaked a pending HTTP request. Test added (`web plugin fetch_url aborts when ctx.signal fires`); assertions for the mock `fetch` call updated to include the signal. Full suite now 161 tests / 7 files passing.

**Rejected as scope creep (do not rework):**

- Drop the redundant "allow empty" flag on `getRequiredString(args, "old_str", true)` in `edit_file` str_replace. Pre-existing Round 1 code, not touched by this round; earlier rounds have consistently rejected this class of micro-cleanup as "not a solidness gap". Fold in whenever the file is next rewritten.
- Remove the `search.length === 0` dead guard in `countOccurrences`. Same reasoning: pre-existing Round 1 code, purely cosmetic, no behavior impact.
- Thread `ctx.signal` into `grep`'s `execFile("rg", …)`. Real but pre-existing Round 2 gap — out of scope for the Round 5 fetch_url review. Added as a dedicated task-board item instead of being silently patched in this round.
- Extending `fetch_url` beyond GET or adding richer response metadata. The plan only calls for arbitrary URL reads with markdown conversion and pagination.
- Folding `fetch_url` into a broader `ToolResult` / multimodal refactor. That is the next task on the board and should stay separate.

**Owner review requested.**

### Round 2 — `grep` / `glob` tools

**Accepted and fixed in this round:**

- Removed no-op `normalizeAllowedPath` proxy (CLAUDE.md "no proxy functions"); `glob` now calls `resolveAllowedPath` directly.
- Updated `edit_file` description to document `overwrite` and `replace_all`.
- Replaced inline `path.relative` expression with the existing `formatDisplayPath` helper.

**Rejected as scope creep (do not rework):**

- Promote local arg-parse helpers (`getOptionalBoolean`, `getOptionalNonNegativeInteger`, `getOptionalGrepOutputMode`) into `plugin-args.ts`. Style cleanup; doesn't make the agent less solid. Pick up when another plugin needs the same helpers.
- Simplify `sliceLines` to `split("\n").slice(...)`. Current impl works, is tested, and avoids splitting the full file into an array. Not a quality gap.
- Drop the "`passed to ripgrep`" phrase from the `grep` glob description. Minor wording; LLM tool selection is unaffected.
- Remove the `Number.isNaN` fallback in `formatGrepOutput`. Defensive code against a future `rg` flag change; harmless.
- Investigator's suggestion to split `files` plugin once it grows is noted — punt until `grep` actually gains streaming.
